📝 Write Composer.test.tsx
/Users/sohamwaychal/Documents/anthropic/apps/web/src/components/bui/Composer.test.tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react'
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'

import { MODEL_ALLOWLIST } from '@/lib/types'

import { Composer, filterScenarios, parseSlash, type ComposerProps } from './Composer'
import { scenarios } from './test-fixtures'

type Spies = Pick<ComposerProps, 'onScenarioChange' | 'onModelChange' | 'onSeedChange' | 'onSubmit'>

/** The component is controlled; this holds the state a page would. */
function Harness({
  spies,
  initialPrompt = '',
  ...rest
}: { spies: Spies; initialPrompt?: string } & Partial<
  Pick<ComposerProps, 'disabled' | 'busy' | 'hint' | 'placeholder'>
>) {
  const [scenarioId, setScenarioId] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [model, setModel] = useState<string>(MODEL_ALLOWLIST[0])
  const [seed, setSeed] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState(initialPrompt)
  return (
    <Composer
      scenarios={scenarios}
      scenarioId={scenarioId}
      onScenarioChange={(id) => {
        setScenarioId(id)
        spies.onScenarioChange(id)
      }}
      models={MODEL_ALLOWLIST}
      model={model}
      onModelChange={(m) => {
        setModel(m)
        spies.onModelChange(m)
      }}
      seed={seed}
      onSeedChange={(s) => {
        setSeed(s)
        spies.onSeedChange(s)
      }}
      prompt={prompt}
      onPromptChange={setPrompt}
      onSubmit={spies.onSubmit}
      {...rest}
    />
  )
}

function spies(): Spies {
  return { onScenarioChange: vi.fn(), onModelChange: vi.fn(), onSeedChange: vi.fn(), onSubmit: vi.fn() }
}

describe('helpers', () => {
  it('parses a slash query only at the start of an otherwise empty draft', () => {
    expect(parseSlash('/')).toBe('')
    expect(parseSlash('/Lost-')).toBe('lost-')
    expect(parseSlash('run /lost')).toBeNull()
    expect(parseSlash('/lost ack')).toBeNull()
    expect(parseSlash('')).toBeNull()
  })

  it('filters scenarios by id prefix or title substring', () => {
    expect(filterScenarios(scenarios, 'lo').map((s) => s.id)).toEqual(['lost-ack'])
    expect(filterScenarios(scenarios, 'file').map((s) => s.id)).toEqual(['missing-file'])
    expect(filterScenarios(scenarios, '')).toHaveLength(3)
  })
})

describe('Composer', () => {
  it('opens the scenario menu on "/" and picks with the arrow keys + Enter', () => {
    const s = spies()
    render(<Harness spies={s} />)
    const input = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Prompt' }) as HTMLTextAreaElement
    expect(screen.queryByRole('listbox')).toBeNull()

    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: '/' } })
    const list = screen.getByRole('listbox', { name: 'Scenarios' })
    const options = screen.getAllByRole('option')
    expect(options).toHaveLength(3)
    expect(options[0]!.getAttribute('aria-selected')).toBe('true')
    expect(list.textContent).toContain('lost-ack')
    expect(list.textContent).toContain('Missing file')

    fireEvent.keyDown(input, { key: 'ArrowDown' })
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('option')[1]!.getAttribute('aria-selected')).toBe('true')
    fireEvent.keyDown(input, { key: 'Enter' })

    expect(s.onScenarioChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith('missing-file')
    expect(s.onSubmit).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(input.value).toBe(scenarios[1]!.task_prompt)
    expect(screen.queryByRole('listbox')).toBeNull()
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Choose scenario' }).textContent).toContain('missing-file')
  })

  it('filters the menu as the query grows and dismisses on Escape', () => {
    const s = spies()
    render(<Harness spies={s} />)
    const input = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Prompt' })
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: '/den' } })
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('option')).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(screen.getByRole('option').textContent).toContain('denied-write')
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: '/zzz' } })
    expect(screen.queryAllByRole('option')).toHaveLength(0)
    expect(screen.getByText(/No scenario matches/)).toBeTruthy()
    fireEvent.keyDown(input, { key: 'Escape' })
    expect(screen.queryByRole('listbox')).toBeNull()
  })

  it('opens the menu from the scenario chip too', () => {
    render(<Harness spies={spies()} />)
    const chip = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Choose scenario' })
    expect(chip.getAttribute('aria-expanded')).toBe('false')
    fireEvent.click(chip)
    expect(chip.getAttribute('aria-expanded')).toBe('true')
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('option')).toHaveLength(3)
    fireEvent.click(screen.getAllByRole('option')[2]!)
    expect(chip.textContent).toContain('denied-write')
  })

  it('submits on Enter, inserts a newline on Shift+Enter, and never with an empty prompt', () => {
    const s = spies()
    render(<Harness spies={s} initialPrompt="Prepare release 0.2.0" />)
    const input = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Prompt' })
    const send = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Send' }) as HTMLButtonElement
    expect(send.disabled).toBe(false)

    fireEvent.keyDown(input, { key: 'Enter', shiftKey: true })
    expect(s.onSubmit).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    fireEvent.keyDown(input, { key: 'Enter' })
    expect(s.onSubmit).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
    fireEvent.click(send)
    expect(s.onSubmit).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)

    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: '   ' } })
    expect(send.disabled).toBe(true)
    fireEvent.keyDown(input, { key: 'Enter' })
    expect(s.onSubmit).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)
  })

  it('respects disabled/busy and shows the hint', () => {
    const s = spies()
    render(<Harness spies={s} initialPrompt="go" disabled hint="harness unreachable" />)
    expect(screen.getByText('harness unreachable')).toBeTruthy()
    const send = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Send' }) as HTMLButtonElement
    expect(send.disabled).toBe(true)
    fireEvent.keyDown(screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Prompt' }), { key: 'Enter' })
    expect(s.onSubmit).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })

  it('shows a running send button while busy', () => {
    render(<Harness spies={spies()} initialPrompt="go" busy />)
    expect((screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Running' }) as HTMLButtonElement).disabled).toBe(true)
  })

  it('exposes the model chip and a numeric seed input', () => {
    const s = spies()
    render(<Harness spies={s} />)
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Choose model' }).textContent).toContain('claude-haiku-4-5')
    const seed = screen.getByLabelText('Seed') as HTMLInputElement
    fireEvent.change(seed, { target: { value: '7' } })
    expect(s.onSeedChange).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(7)
    expect(seed.value).toBe('7')
    fireEvent.change(seed, { target: { value: '' } })
    expect(s.onSeedChange).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(null)
  })
})

function header(r: HTMLElement): HTMLElement {

function toggleOf(r: HTMLElement): HTMLElement {

📝 Write check-css.mjs
/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-sohamwaychal-Documents-anthropic/bdece89f-be6c-4103-b139-3f422caef4d5/scratchpad/check-css.mjs
// Compile apps/web/src/index.css with the real Tailwind v4 pipeline and check that the
// Beautiful UI token bridge + motion classes used by src/components/bui actually generate.
import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import path from 'node:path'

const app = '/Users/sohamwaychal/Documents/anthropic/apps/web'
const pnpm = path.join(app, 'node_modules/.pnpm')
const viteDir = readdirSync(pnpm).find((d) => d.startsWith('@tailwindcss+vite@'))
const nodePkg = path.join(pnpm, viteDir, 'node_modules/@tailwindcss/node/dist/index.mjs')
const { compile } = await import(nodePkg)

const css = readFileSync(path.join(app, 'src/index.css'), 'utf8')
const compiler = await compile(css, { base: path.join(app, 'src'), onDependency: () => {} })

const candidates = [
  'text-ink', 'text-ink-2', 'text-ink-3', 'bg-ink', 'bg-ink-3', 'border-t-ink-2',
  'bg-canvas', 'text-canvas', 'bg-surface', 'border-line', 'border-line-strong', 'bg-line-strong', 'ring-line',
  'bg-brand', 'text-brand', 'text-brand-ink', 'bg-brand-tint',
  'motion-safe:transition-[grid-template-rows,opacity]', 'motion-safe:duration-400', 'motion-safe:duration-300',
  'motion-safe:ease-[cubic-bezier(0.23,1,0.32,1)]', 'grid-rows-[0fr]', 'grid-rows-[1fr]',
  'h-5.5', 'size-5.5', 'opacity-15', 'decoration-rose-500/50', 'bg-emerald-500/[0.06]',
  'aria-expanded:bg-muted/60', 'group-hover/row:opacity-0', 'focus-visible:ring-ring/50',
]
const out = compiler.build(candidates)

const esc = (s) => s.replace(/[.:/[\],()]/g, (c) => `\\${c}`)
const missing = candidates.filter((c) => !out.includes(`.${esc(c)}`))
const plain = ['@keyframes bui-pop-in', '@keyframes bui-shimmer-text', '.bui-shimmer', '.bui-pixel', '.bui-fade-up', 'prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference']
const missingPlain = plain.filter((p) => !out.includes(p))

// the bridge must resolve to shadcn vars, not to literal colours
const inkRule = out.match(/\.text-ink\s*\{[^}]*\}/)?.[0] ?? '(no .text-ink rule)'
const brandTint = out.match(/\.bg-brand-tint\s*\{[^}]*\}/)?.[0] ?? '(no .bg-brand-tint rule)'

console.log('missing utilities:', missing.length ? missing : 'none')
console.log('missing plain css:', missingPlain.length ? missingPlain : 'none')
console.log(inkRule)
console.log(brandTint)
console.log('bytes:', out.length)
process.exit(missing.length || missingPlain.length ? 1 : 0)

  --color-ink-2: color-mix(in oklch, var(--foreground) 75%, transparent);
  --color-ink-3: var(--muted-foreground);

/* Beautiful UI motion (src/components/bui). One reduced-motion switch for every ported
   component: outside `no-preference` these classes are inert and the shimmer is static text.
   Transitions in the components use Tailwind's `motion-safe:` variant for the same reason. */
@keyframes bui-pop-in {
  from { opacity: 0; transform: scale(0.96); }
  to { opacity: 1; transform: scale(1); }
}
@keyframes bui-fade-in {
  from { opacity: 0; }
  to { opacity: 1; }
}
@keyframes bui-fade-up {
  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(4px); }
  to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
}
@keyframes bui-shimmer-text {
  from { background-position: 200% 0; }
  to { background-position: -200% 0; }
}
@keyframes bui-pixel-on {
  0%, 100% { opacity: 0.15; }
  50% { opacity: 1; }
}
@keyframes bui-spin {
  to { transform: rotate(360deg); }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference) {
  .bui-pop-in { animation: bui-pop-in 250ms cubic-bezier(0.23, 1, 0.32, 1) both; }
  .bui-fade-in { animation: bui-fade-in 300ms ease-out both; }
  .bui-fade-up { animation: bui-fade-up 320ms cubic-bezier(0.23, 1, 0.32, 1) both; }
  .bui-spin { animation: bui-spin 700ms linear infinite; }
  .bui-pixel { animation: bui-pixel-on var(--bui-dur, 650ms) ease-in-out var(--bui-delay, 0ms) infinite; }
  .bui-shimmer {
    color: transparent;
    -webkit-background-clip: text;
    background-clip: text;
    background-image: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--muted-foreground) 35%, var(--foreground) 50%, var(--muted-foreground) 65%);
    background-size: 200% 100%;
    animation: bui-shimmer-text 1.4s linear infinite;
  }
}

Exported API (from @/components/bui)

AssistantText({ text: string; streaming?: boolean; className?: string })
ThinkingTrace({ step: number; maxSteps?: number; calls: ToolCallView[]; thinking?: string | null;
                active: boolean; done: boolean; defaultOpen?: boolean; children?: ReactNode })
ToolCallChips({ calls: ToolCallView[]; diffs?: FileDiff[]; live?: boolean })
CodeBlock({ code?: string; diff?: string; filename?: string; language?: string; maxHeight?: number; className?: string })
ScoreRows({ evaluation: EvaluateResponse })
LoadingState({ label: string; sublabel?: string; className?: string })
FileStatusTable({ files: FileEntry[]; onSelect?: (path: string) => void; selected?: string | null })
Composer({ scenarios: Scenario[]; scenarioId: string | null; onScenarioChange: (id: string) => void;
           models: readonly string[]; model: string; onModelChange: (m: string) => void;
           seed: number | null; onSeedChange: (s: number | null) => void;
           prompt: string; onPromptChange: (p: string) => void; onSubmit: () => void;
           disabled?: boolean; busy?: boolean; placeholder?: string; hint?: string })

Also exported helpers: parseUnifiedDiff, DiffLine, parseSlash, filterScenarios, sortFiles, scoreTone, summariseCall, diffForCall, resultParts, toolLabel, TOOL_ICON, TOOL_LABEL, and the *Props types.

Simplifications / decisions vs. the originals

  • ToolCallChips: expansion is inline (grid-rows reveal) rather than the hover portal, so it works in a scrolling column; no outer "N tool calls" header (ThinkingTrace provides it). Rows carry +n −m counts when a diff matches; matching uses normalisePath for path tools and callTouches for mutating shell commands. Result parts are identical to Timeline.tsx (error+code, stdout, stderr, content, entries, bytes written, truncated), with content rendered through CodeBlock.
  • CodeBlock: takes raw strings and parses unified diffs itself (---/+++ meta only before the first hunk; @@ drives numbering). fetch gone; clipboard guarded. Keyword tint extended with Python; language="text|plain|log" disables it. accent-ink keyword colour replaced by sky-300 because brand-ink (= primary-foreground) would be invisible on a card in dark mode.
  • Composer: @ sources, attachments, dictation and glimm removed; slash menu is inline with role=listbox/option, sliding highlight kept. Model chip is shadcn DropdownMenu (radio group). disabled gates sending only — typing, / picking, model and seed stay usable. I chose this because HomePage.tsx (written concurrently by another process) passes disabled={!canRun || !scenario}; disabling the textarea would have made it impossible to pick the first scenario.
  • AssistantText: cursor kept; the last line shimmers while streaming; `code`/**bold** styled inline; sources/follow-ups dropped.
  • ThinkingTrace/ScoreRows/LoadingState/FileStatusTable: demo timers, the "Surfer" video, and DiffTable's toggle/apply flow removed. Status glyphs are labelled spans (role="img") since lucide icons don't expose aria-label reliably.
  • Semantic colours (green/red/orange) map to the app's emerald/rose/amber; hover/inset/field surfaces map to bg-muted/*. Collapsed panels use aria-hidden + inert.

Integration note

Another process was concurrently rewriting the app and already imports my exports: src/components/transcript/TranscriptView.tsx (AssistantText, LoadingState, ScoreRows, ThinkingTrace, ToolCallChips), src/components/workspace/WorkspaceColumn.tsx (CodeBlock, FileStatusTable), src/pages/HomePage.tsx and src/pages/ConversationPage.tsx (Composer). Their usage type-checks against my props.

  • pnpm exec tsc -p tsconfig.app.json --noEmit → 0 errors (project-wide; an isolated bui/-only tsconfig also exits 0).
  • pnpm exec vitest run → Test Files 15 passed, Tests 146 passed (original 44 green + my 41 across 8 files + the other process's new tests).
  • Compiled src/index.css through the real Tailwind pipeline (@tailwindcss/node): every bui utility generates (.text-ink { color: var(--foreground) }, bg-brand-tint → color-mix with @supports fallback, motion-safe:*, grid-rows-[0fr], h-5.5, etc.), and the keyframes / reduced-motion block are present.
